# Add 63 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

### Identity (reviewed)

Dave McMurtrie's assembly reconstruction of Space Invaders for the Commodore PET. Hardware and game variables are named in a substantial single source listing.
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/blob/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da/README.md
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/blob/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da/commodore_pet_invaders.asm
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/commit/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da

### Classification (reviewed)

2,093-line actual listing includes PET kernal calls, PIA/VIA locations, alien/game logic and data. Original-code completeness and exact binary identity not independently verified. No licence file or explicit reuse grant found in pinned repository. Public availability alone is not a licence.
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/blob/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da/README.md
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/blob/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da/commodore_pet_invaders.asm
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/commit/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Original subject/guest architecture, rather than the compiler host.
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/blob/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da/README.md
- https://github.com/commodoreihs/PET_Invaders_Disassembly/blob/47d2488212307c0ad8049ae5f1f57d913bbd91da/commodore_pet_invaders.asm

### Build (reviewed)

No build script; source uses .byte/.null/format directives and BASIC SYS loader at $0401. Exact assembler invocation not documented. No upstream compilation or execution performed; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown. No candidate software was compiled or executed; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.

### Identity (reviewed)

Binary patch and link-time override layer that adapts the stock Amiga UNIX SVR4 2.1c kernel from its original 68020/68030 MMU assumptions to 68040/68060 processors. The substantial assembly implements page-table/HAT translation, exception and cache handling, boot/runtime changes and support glue; this is not a complete recovered kernel distribution.
- https://github.com/asokero/amix-040-060-port/blob/fe814cb6abdca1a91ae29523ef08efb681c77c5d/README.md
- https://github.com/asokero/amix-040-060-port/blob/fe814cb6abdca1a91ae29523ef08efb681c77c5d/BUILDING.md
- https://github.com/asokero/amix-040-060-port/blob/fe814cb6abdca1a91ae29523ef08efb681c77c5d/src/hat040.s
- https://github.com/asokero/amix-040-060-port/blob/fe814cb6abdca1a91ae29523ef08efb681c77c5d/src/pstart040.s
- https://github.com/asokero/amix-040-060-port/blob/fe814cb6abdca1a91ae29523ef08efb681c77c5d/src/fpsp060_glue.s
- https://github.com/asokero/amix-040-060-port/blob/fe814cb6abdca1a91ae29523ef08efb681c77c5d/LICENSE
- https://github.com/asokero/amix-040-060-port/commit/fe814cb6abdca1a91ae29523ef08efb681c77c5d

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected actual HAT assembly with original addresses and documented 030-to-040 descriptor conversions. Extensive diagnostic/test and contract documentation; hardware acceptance claims are upstream reports only. MIT for new code/modifications. Original AMIX is proprietary and supplied by user. Motorola packages retain separate terms and NetBSD-derived algorithms BSD notices. Source explicitly acknowledges identical one-line vendor ABI macros.

### Identity (reviewed)

Clean-room C99 reimplementation of Electronic Arts’ 1988 Amiga flight simulator. Recovered behaviour notes drive separate flight, world, combat, game-flow and audio implementations, with SDL2 native and browser front ends and a bring-your-own-disk extractor.
- https://github.com/st-gr/interceptor-port/blob/2475dcd65f454a37b41a2be27629ad958f34ddc5/README.md

- https://github.com/st-gr/interceptor-port/commit/2475dcd65f454a37b41a2be27629ad958f34ddc5

### Classification (reviewed)

Flight source preserves 16-bit arithmetic conventions, documents address-indexed table lookups and accepted divergences from recovered notes. Large core/test tree and explicit clean-room scanner limits. GPL-3.0. Reference tooling builds on GPL vAmiga; original game/data remain EA copyright and are not distributed.

### Identity (reviewed)

Study and reconstruction of Aura’s 1992 Atari ST/STE music disk, including extracted/disassembled original 68000 code, a platform-neutral behavioural specification, a TypeScript browser port, C/SDL3 desktop port and native Amiga 500 assembly conversion.

### Identity (reviewed)

Portable C compression/decompression library implementing the bit-packed history-copy format used by Gordon Key’s DeComp/FDComp modules in Fourth Dimension and Fednet Acorn games. Includes streaming ring-buffer codec, independent compressor and tests, rather than being only a format note.
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/GKeyLib/blob/948ed97b5e9e5ad9894a62bfacff4db30332cc99/README.md
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/GKeyLib/blob/948ed97b5e9e5ad9894a62bfacff4db30332cc99/GKeyDecomp.c

- https://github.com/chrisbazley/GKeyLib/blob/948ed97b5e9e5ad9894a62bfacff4db30332cc99/LICENSE
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/GKeyLib/commit/948ed97b5e9e5ad9894a62bfacff4db30332cc99

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual decompressor state machine and compressor/ring-buffer sources inspected; README documents exact packed fields and original module bugs. Existing CBLibrary’s mention of dependency is not a standalone GKeyLib entry. LGPL-2.1-or-later per source headers and README; LICENSE contains LGPL 2.1.

### Identity (reviewed)

Two C command-line tools recover Star Fighter 3000’s native object-mesh definitions and mission palettes into Wavefront OBJ/MTL. The parser reconstructs indexed vertices, plot primitives, clipping/grouping and colour/material semantics from original game files.
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/SF3KtoObj/blob/b34fb4ab380aec9fd0b4b06d25702ac0dd30aeb5/README.md
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/SF3KtoObj/blob/b34fb4ab380aec9fd0b4b06d25702ac0dd30aeb5/parser.c

### Identity (reviewed)

C converter that locates and decodes the object-model tables embedded in Apocalypse’s APCOD game executable and writes Wavefront OBJ meshes and MTL colour references. Distinct game-specific parser for Gordon Key’s 1990 Acorn Archimedes title.
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/ApocToObj/blob/f36160d0ea6d87b3a1099cdd7e440f2c508fc957/README.md
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/ApocToObj/blob/f36160d0ea6d87b3a1099cdd7e440f2c508fc957/parser.c
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/ApocToObj/blob/f36160d0ea6d87b3a1099cdd7e440f2c508fc957/apoctoobj.c

- https://github.com/chrisbazley/ApocToObj/blob/f36160d0ea6d87b3a1099cdd7e440f2c508fc957/LICENSE
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/ApocToObj/commit/f36160d0ea6d87b3a1099cdd7e440f2c508fc957

### Classification (reviewed)

Parser contains executable load address 0x8f00, object-count/primitive structures, back-face and vertex reconstruction. README documents APCOD extraction and named-object conversion. GPL-2.0-or-later per parser headers and README. Original Apocalypse APCOD remains required input; tool license does not cover game.
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/ApocToObj/blob/f36160d0ea6d87b3a1099cdd7e440f2c508fc957/README.md
- https://github.com/chrisbazley/ApocToObj/blob/f36160d0ea6d87b3a1099cdd7e440f2c508fc957/parser.c

APCOD is an ARM executable, though tool extracts embedded model data rather than translating ARM instructions.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original native classic-Mac Gopher application with MacTCP transport, Gopher/Gopher+ protocol parser, multi-window browsing, favorites, file downloads, text/HTML processing and System 6/7 display integration. Full, Lite and Minimal variants target constrained 68000 machines.
- https://github.com/ecliptik/geomys/blob/8358881bf90d27c9d9df8377fd464f457feef17c/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

### Identity (reviewed)

Integrated X68000 cross-development environment built around m68k GCC/binutils, Human68k/IOCS runtime support and ELF-to-X executable plus X68k-object-to-ELF converters. Also supplies startup/linker/spec files, Newlib integration, socket and pthread compatibility libraries and remote-debug wrappers.
- https://github.com/yunkya2/elf2x68k/blob/5e92bd6c1f00902cdbcbe28bf51e3f549670ef39/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Human68k loopback block-device driver and losetup command that mount XDF/DIM/D88 floppy or HDF/HDS/MOS storage images as local drives. Implements resident driver installation plus a documented patch to Human68k disk-I/O cache linked-list handling to permit nested filesystem access.
- https://github.com/yunkya2/loopdrv-x68k/blob/b826f39469a4ffabf5711588e0e8d7f38eb61535/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Work-in-progress Atari STE enhancement project for Lotus Turbo Challenge 2, with address-specific original routine stubs, replacement road/background/car rendering, blitter-assisted sprite paths and runtime caching. Build emits patch blocks keyed to original game addresses rather than a standalone replacement game.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reverse-engineered reimplementation of Brøderbund’s 1990 Amiga Wings of Fury for Atari STE/Mega STE. A substantial C++ game implements original flight model, enemies, weapons, all 15 maps, front end, saved games and drawing on AGT, with Amiga-format decoders and conversion scripts plus 68k disassembly/decompilation research.
- https://github.com/theimann/wings-of-fury-atari-ste/blob/c6fd48298109b5148dd4837aadb0e78082cbc2a7/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected actual ~7400-line native game source with fixed 12.5Hz original-model logic, original-address table references and MiNT/AGT hardware integration. Fifty-one recorded input test flights documented. MIT covers conversion’s own code/tools/tests/docs; original Brøderbund assets/program, derived graphics and AGT diff excluded. Repository contains original/derived game materials; no blanket open-license claim.

### Identity (reviewed)

Community continuation of Juan Antonio Gómez Gálvez’s reusable MSX/MSX2/MSX2+ emulator for Amiga, built from original v3.1 source and parts of v3.2/v3.5. Large native 68020 assembly implementation includes Z80 execution, VDP timing/sprites, cartridge mappers, FM sound, menus and save support.
- https://github.com/z00p/AmiMSX/blob/dce6b9ed18d2836c07bc4d1ae3bcd25f603f8b01/README.md
- https://github.com/z00p/AmiMSX/blob/dce6b9ed18d2836c07bc4d1ae3bcd25f603f8b01/AmiMSX_Main_v3.1.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual main assembler documents Z80 cycles, PAL/NTSC scanline timing, V9958 behaviour, mapper handling and split sprite snapshots. Generic multisystem emulator, not a game-specific CPU wrapper. No broad main-code license found. README says original author granted community-update permission; that is not an OSI license. emu2413 FM instrument data carries separate MIT terms; system ROM/data rights distinct.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original Dreamcast first-person shooter and custom DCE3D engine by Josh PH3NOM Pearson. The published source includes a native PowerVR rendering pipeline, player/enemy combat, collision, scene triggers, custom MD2 loading and FMV support. This is the released incomplete native game project, not a commercial-game reconstruction.
- https://github.com/PH3NOM-PRO/in-the-line-of-fire/blob/bbd6267487b7d038873467a73775564e3ccb5fca/README.md
- https://github.com/PH3NOM-PRO/in-the-line-of-fire/blob/bbd6267487b7d038873467a73775564e3ccb5fca/src/Makefile
- https://github.com/PH3NOM-PRO/in-the-line-of-fire/blob/bbd6267487b7d038873467a73775564e3ccb5fca/src/dce/dce-main.c
- https://github.com/PH3NOM-PRO/in-the-line-of-fire/blob/bbd6267487b7d038873467a73775564e3ccb5fca/src/dce/dce-enemy.c
- https://github.com/PH3NOM-PRO/in-the-line-of-fire/commit/bbd6267487b7d038873467a73775564e3ccb5fca

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantial implementation inspected in dce-main.c and dce-enemy.c; README explicitly says only the first level currently runs and cleanup/refactoring remain. Original Blender exporter for the custom higher-precision MD2 format was lost. Pinned README explicitly releases source under GNU GPL and links GPLv3. Bundled decoder code has its own upstream terms; the README is the project-level licence evidence, not a blanket relicensing of all media.
- https://github.com/PH3NOM-PRO/in-the-line-of-fire/blob/bbd6267487b7d038873467a73775564e3ccb5fca/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

XL2 Sonic Z-Treme is a native Sega Saturn Sonic fangame using an older Z-Treme Engine, with game/entity logic, player physics, camera/rendering, CD loading, animation and sound source. It is an independently programmed fangame inspired by Sonic X-Treme, not a recovered Sonic X-Treme source tree.
- https://github.com/abelbmx/SONIC-Z-TREME/blob/cff75451c1616aac1236fc2b44223902b55c706b/README.md
- https://github.com/abelbmx/SONIC-Z-TREME/blob/cff75451c1616aac1236fc2b44223902b55c706b/LICENSE

- https://github.com/abelbmx/SONIC-Z-TREME/commit/cff75451c1616aac1236fc2b44223902b55c706b

### Classification (reviewed)

Native SRC/game.c and build graph inspected. README identifies this as v0.0813 and says the SAGE demo is a better playable build than the source revision; author explicitly describes code-quality limitations. GPLv3 LICENSE present for project code. README separately forbids selling the game unless Sonic/audio/music/texture assets and Sega SBL/SGL are removed, so proprietary assets and bundled SDK components must not be represented as GPL-cleared.

### Identity (reviewed)

High-level C development library and Windows-oriented toolchain for Neo Geo CD, layered over Neo Dev Kit and DATlib. Its own implementation manages sprites, palettes, joypads, collisions, CDDA/ADPCM and project workflows, rather than merely collecting links or wrapping one game.
- https://github.com/David-Vandensteen/neocore/blob/f1875d30e4caf88deca9cce4e301f10ae4c85b83/README.md

Inspected actual src-lib/neocore.c as well as a bullet sample and native library/project makefiles. Library includes original high-level managers over DATlib; no claim of binary reconstruction or byte matching. README displays an MIT licence badge, but recursive pinned tree has no standalone licence file. Treat as an upstream MIT claim with incomplete licence text evidence; bundled Neo Dev Kit/DATlib and external tools retain independent terms.

### Identity (reviewed)

Enhanced C++ recreation of the Homestar Runner Flash game Trogdor. README explicitly says the author opened the original in a Flash decompiler and manually ported its ActionScript and assets to C++, recreating Flash animation/scaling behavior. Includes real gameplay classes, collision/animation logic and multiple console builds, not an emulator wrapper.
- https://github.com/Mode8fx/Trogdor-Reburninated/blob/2b6a82a7a44fcab1584b09d7d41fd648d7d28f7f/README.md
- https://github.com/Mode8fx/Trogdor-Reburninated/blob/2b6a82a7a44fcab1584b09d7d41fd648d7d28f7f/LICENSE
- https://github.com/Mode8fx/Trogdor-Reburninated/blob/2b6a82a7a44fcab1584b09d7d41fd648d7d28f7f/Trogdor-Reburninated/Makefiles/Makefile_gc

- https://github.com/Mode8fx/Trogdor-Reburninated/commit/2b6a82a7a44fcab1584b09d7d41fd648d7d28f7f

### Classification (reviewed)

Clear decompilation-derived lineage with translated implementation. README lists expanded gameplay/difficulty, saves, cheats, intermissions and configurable scaling; those are upstream claims, not independently verified. Avoid inferring byte-exactness from faithful-recreation wording. MIT LICENSE for new source. README says most art/audio are taken from original Flash and identifies Brothers Chaps ownership and Jonathan Howe original programming; MIT must not be read as clearing those assets.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original C++ SDL1.2/SDL2 Sudoku game with on-demand puzzle generation, pre-generated harder puzzles, controller/touch/mouse input, saves and scalable graphics. Includes extensive native console build support and its own game-logic implementation; no reverse-engineering origin is claimed.

### Identity (reviewed)

Independent C# WonderSwan/Color emulator with its own V30MZ core, hardware devices and desktop interfaces. A Windows Forms player and Dear ImGui/OpenGL debugger/disassembler share the core; this is reusable console emulation and inspection tooling, not a game-specific runtime.
- https://github.com/xdanieldzd/StoicGoose/blob/e6a7c93cedbb50f098262cd17824e3f9e577caad/README.md

CPU arithmetic/flag implementation in V30MZ.Implementations.cs inspected; tree contains DMA, display, sound, EEPROM and cartridge devices plus breakpoint/debugger components. Work-in-progress status and Linux testing limited to WSL are explicit upstream caveats. MIT LICENSE.md inspected.

### Identity (reviewed)

Cycle-stepped Atari Lynx emulator library in Rust, exposing Lynx CPU/bus, cartridge/EEPROM, Mikey display/audio/UART and Suzy sprite/math hardware. Its reusable core serves several separately packaged frontends, rather than embedding emulation for one game.
- https://github.com/LLeny/holani/blob/7871f5cc6a26cf458f1adaaf54c4ccbd3ab3e338/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual Lynx device composition and cycle-stepped CPU macros/instruction steps inspected. README credits chips/floooh design inspiration for CPU emulation; no equivalence to a wrapper around chips is inferred. GPLv3 LICENSE present. README explicitly notes embedded Free Lynx Boot ROM and BLL uLoader blobs with source credits; these deserve separate provenance review before redistribution.

### Identity (reviewed)

Long-running reusable PC Engine/TurboGrafx-16 emulator for Nintendo DS-family hardware, with its own native emulation loop, HuC6280 integration, VDC/VCE/PSG state, CD-ROM and Arcade Card handling. Distinct target-specific emulator in FluBBa library family.
- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/blob/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0/README.md
- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/blob/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0/Makefile
- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/blob/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0/source/cpu.s
- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/blob/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0/source/PCEngine.c
- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/commit/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0

### Classification (reviewed)

Pinned head fixes an Arcade Card regression. README explicitly warns compatibility is imperfect and that SuperGrafx is not emulated. Frame-step and debug graphics toggles exist; no instruction debugger claim made. No project-level LICENSE/COPYING file in inspected pinned tree; source cpu.s says all rights reserved. Makefile is CC0 and bundled zlib/CUEParser code has separate terms. Do not infer an overall open-source licence from public source availability.
- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/blob/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0/README.md
- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/blob/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0/Makefile

- https://github.com/FluBBaOfWard/NitroGrafx/commit/502c4ce71c966fdf1ea5aa625428aa26bcfffcd0

Input/emulated architecture only; not the compiler host.

### Build (reviewed)

BlocksDS/Wonderful Makefile produces NitroGrafx.nds using ARM9 libnds/maxmod and an ARM7 support ELF. cpu.s executes emulated HuC6280 cycles and display scanlines; PCEngine.c serializes RAM/video/CPU/chip state. No build or upstream software execution was performed; all build flags remain unknown. No candidate software was compiled or executed; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.

NDS native ARM9 code plus ARM7 support. 3DS mention is DS-mode use, not a native ARM11 3DS build. Requires legally supplied games, and CD-ROM use requires selected BIOS. No runtime verified.

### Identity (reviewed)

Jolly Good API educational emulator for ColecoVision/Super Game Module, CreatiVision and My Vision, with documented hardware models, memory mapping, state serialization, controller devices and TMS9918/PSG integration. This libretro-hosted source also includes an actual reusable core and JG API build.
- https://github.com/libretro/jollycv/blob/2790d59c7194d29e83f8603ffd7e9da17f11575e/README
- https://github.com/libretro/jollycv/blob/2790d59c7194d29e83f8603ffd7e9da17f11575e/LICENSE
- https://github.com/libretro/jollycv/blob/2790d59c7194d29e83f8603ffd7e9da17f11575e/LICENSES

- https://github.com/libretro/jollycv/commit/2790d59c7194d29e83f8603ffd7e9da17f11575e

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected src/jcv_coleco.c state/controller/memory implementation, not just adapter. README describes CreatiVision support as experimental; 100% ColecoVision compatibility is upstream-reported and not independently measured. BSD-3-Clause project LICENSE. LICENSES separately enumerates MIT Jolly Good Z80 and m6502 components plus Speex resampler terms.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native WonderSwan hardware-behavior and emulator validation test ROM collection, covering CPU quirks, timing, DMA, display, EEPROM, interrupts, sound and RTC, with interactive timing/memory/I/O inspection utilities. Substantial C/assembly tests and own ROM build graph rather than a list of tests elsewhere.
- https://github.com/asiekierka/ws-test-suite/blob/7dfa0e2e869d08386b685d6a56df0bcfaf181b47/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual test implementation inspected. README says per-test documentation is incomplete and result marks appear right-to-left; source is required to interpret many results. Tests are designed to investigate hardware, not proof that all emulator implementations pass. MIT project LICENSE; build script is explicitly CC0-1.0.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original GameCube/Wii homebrew hovercraft racing project inspired by Diddy Kong Racing. Source includes component-based scenes, controller input, hovercraft behavior, camera/math, graphics/resources and rendering systems. It is an independently written native racer prototype, not a port or Diddy Kong Racing reconstruction.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable native Nintendo DS emulator for WonderSwan, Color/Crystal and Benesse PocketChallenge V2, with V30MZ execution, hardware scanline integration, cartridge/EEPROM save states and ROM loading. Its WonderWitch/BootFriend transfer tools also support handheld development workflows.

### Identity (reviewed)

The 1994 Safari Software space-combat game reconstructed from its shipped binary into a modular C engine. Flight/combat implementation cites original byte offsets; DOS graphics, cockpit, missions, keyboard, music and sound drivers are present. It targets semantic equivalence rather than byte-identical recompilation.

113 C/Python/assembly source files reviewed at tree level. Repository reports ten missions and 41 gates including execution of original code; these are upstream claims, not independently rerun. Reconstruction is PolyForm Noncommercial 1.0.0. ### Identity (reviewed)

A reusable analysis, disassembly, x86-to-C lifting and runtime toolkit extracted from multiple PC recompilation projects. Includes MZ/NE/LE/PE and NeXTSTEP Mach-O readers; x86-16/32/64 lifters and differential tests; DOS, Win16, Win32-on-POSIX and native32 runtime components. It is infrastructure rather than a game-specific interpreter wrapper.
- https://github.com/sp00nznet/pcrecomp/blob/bd26806fbd4714d670d2dee75973c5eaff64674c/README.md
- https://github.com/sp00nznet/pcrecomp/blob/bd26806fbd4714d670d2dee75973c5eaff64674c/tools/lift/lift16.py

### Classification (reviewed)

157 source files across analysis/lifting tools; actual lift16 operand-to-CPU-C emission inspected. Pinned fix handles overlapping REP MOVS semantics. Runtime completeness varies; Win16 link stubs are explicitly incomplete. MIT project licence; binary assets, third-party SDKs and external disassembler databases are not thereby relicensed.

### Identity (reviewed)

A separate osFree subproject reimplementing the 16-bit Windows 3.0 environment on DOS/VDM, with kernel, USER, loader, drivers and system components. Source incorporates and adapts Wine, TWIN, ReactOS and HX-DOS code. KRNL286 selector allocation and its Watcom/JWasm build are present, not merely a compatibility proposal.
- https://github.com/osfree-project/WIN16/blob/094ac8abb0b66a42037a0b34810102dfc4e9b9e3/README.MD
- https://github.com/osfree-project/WIN16/blob/094ac8abb0b66a42037a0b34810102dfc4e9b9e3/core/kernel/selector.c

- https://github.com/osfree-project/WIN16/commit/094ac8abb0b66a42037a0b34810102dfc4e9b9e3

### Classification (reviewed)

864 C/assembly source files including archived reference trees, so file count is not a completeness measure. README explicitly says very early alpha, GDI unimplemented, and first goal is simple WinVer/Clock startup. Root licence is LGPL-2.1-or-later and refers to Wine origins; other inherited components carry their own GPL/BSD/LGPL terms. Do not treat the entire tree as uniformly licensed.

### Identity (reviewed)

Independent osFree Family API implementation for binary dual-mode applications that run without recompilation on DOS and OS/2. Contains KAL, VIO/KBD/MOU, memory/file/NLS, static and dynamic API libraries, and a Wine-derived NE DOS loader.
- https://github.com/osfree-project/FamilyAPI/blob/b2d26b366d3d0251cdf366032e2f788337894b07/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Continuation of Rene Straub's 1995–1996 Tetris-style OS/2 Presentation Manager game. The maintained 1.50 tree retains the historical playfield engine and adds multilingual UI/help, configurable input, sound and modern Open Watcom builds; legacy source/material is separated.
- https://github.com/OS2World/GAME-PUZZLE-STris/blob/f597bb477d6bb5b77bf3c1c6742aabd810c68dfb/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Original source of Media Pocket's 1999 Brick Blaster and WinEOS 4.00 Alpha, restored to match the released game by comparing disassemblies. Includes translation-length/coordinate corrections, updated makefiles and narrowly documented compatibility patches for Windows.
- https://github.com/david4599/BrickBlaster/blob/aa049716d55a434ed6f4fd677bb79c45ed29b778/README.md
- https://github.com/david4599/BrickBlaster/blob/aa049716d55a434ed6f4fd677bb79c45ed29b778/work400/Blaster/MAIN.ASM

- https://github.com/david4599/BrickBlaster/commit/aa049716d55a434ed6f4fd677bb79c45ed29b778

### Classification (reviewed)

42 C/assembly files including the complete assembly game and WinEOS services. Distinct from the unrelated Amstrad CPC Brick Blaster already tracked. GPL-3.0 text at root. Historical proprietary compiler/helper binaries and original game assets require their separate rights; source availability does not relicense toolchain packages.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original open-source release and continued maintenance of the 1997 first-person role-playing game. The large C engine preserves DOS renderer/game/network modules alongside SDL-based native ports and a newly integrated Emscripten target.

### Identity (reviewed)

A substantive Commander Keen 4 EGA source modification derived from K1n9_Duk3's reconstructed Keen 4–6 sources. Changes the engine timing/render pipeline to 70-fps operation, adds smooth one-pixel panning options and CRT Terminator synchronization support, while retaining the reconstructed C/assembly game tree.
- https://github.com/juj/KEEN70HZ/blob/b87a0dd4a1c9423eac8a9f61192ac251ae811dce/README.md
- https://github.com/juj/KEEN70HZ/blob/b87a0dd4a1c9423eac8a9f61192ac251ae811dce/KEEN4-6/ID_RF.C

### Classification (reviewed)

58 C/assembly files. Specific engine changes are meaningful; does not claim the upstream 4–6 reconstruction as a newly discovered separate project. Last project commit is 2024. Reconstructed source headers say GPL-2.0-or-later; bundled Keen 4 data is Apogee shareware and Borland toolchain is proprietary. Mixed repository contents must be distinguished.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native homebrew DOS game-engine framework with VGA Mode 13h double buffering, compiled x86 sprite blitting, Korean/Latin bitmap fonts, PIT timing, keyboard IRQs, OPL2 VGM playback and Sound Blaster PCM mixing. Includes an actual game/smoke-test harness and TypeScript asset tools.
- https://github.com/gcjjyy/watcom-game-dev/blob/a0c25f91c4fd71b52331158e4f3687639a77ae68/README.md
- https://github.com/gcjjyy/watcom-game-dev/blob/a0c25f91c4fd71b52331158e4f3687639a77ae68/SRC/GFX.CPP

- https://github.com/gcjjyy/watcom-game-dev/commit/a0c25f91c4fd71b52331158e4f3687639a77ae68

### Classification (reviewed)

14 core C++ source files plus asset tools. Directly inspected VGA copy primitives use EDI/ESI/ECX and Watcom pragma assembly, supporting x86 DOS classification. It is a framework, not a historical reconstruction. MIT for authored engine source. Bundled Watcom files are a separate historical compiler distribution and not automatically MIT.

### Identity (reviewed)

Historical archive by an original author augmented with concrete Space Panicco technical analysis and a working browser VMAP level editor. Promoted for implemented tooling and format analysis, not as recovered game source.

### Identity (reviewed)

A native Turbo Pascal DOS action game made for the 2026 CGA Game Jam. Its nine Pascal modules implement timed gameplay, player/enemy behavior, HUD, sprites, scoring, background and menu, using the CGA BGI driver.

Nine Pascal modules and original graphics source assets. Concrete playable game source rather than an idea/demo-only placeholder. No standard project licence file or explicit reuse grant observed; public source does not imply an open-source licence. CGA BGI driver has separate historical rights.

### Identity (reviewed)

A reusable Python-standard-library memory scan/edit/freeze tool for DOSBox Staging's HTTP memory API. A local Python server serves its JavaScript UI, scans memory and maintains cheat tables; it targets arbitrary DOS software rather than wrapping one game.
- https://github.com/tethragon/DOSBox-Cheat-Engine/blob/7b0f89877e4377c2697a4fddf2e59edabe150892/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual source includes API requests, scan narrowing, writes/freezing, JSON cheat-table persistence and browser-beacon shutdown. Not a CPU emulator or game-specific execution wrapper. No LICENSE file or explicit standard software licence found in inspected root/source. Reuse rights remain unclear.

### Identity (reviewed)

A clean-room NES reimplementation of Commander Keen Galaxy episodes 4–6 with a PC-side pipeline extracting the original DOS executable tables, graphics, maps and audio into NES assets. Native C/6502 game code targets MMC5, with rescaled 60-Hz player physics, actor logic, HUD, audio and banked graphics.
- https://github.com/ronaldstoner/keen-nes/blob/70da871fff11c99c3a8a8421a91183ac376777a2/README.md
- https://github.com/ronaldstoner/keen-nes/blob/70da871fff11c99c3a8a8421a91183ac376777a2/src/player.c
- https://github.com/ronaldstoner/keen-nes/blob/70da871fff11c99c3a8a8421a91183ac376777a2/src/main.c

- https://github.com/ronaldstoner/keen-nes/commit/70da871fff11c99c3a8a8421a91183ac376777a2

### Classification (reviewed)

29 C/assembly implementation files; actual player physics documents conversion of original 70-Hz behavior to NES 60 Hz. README now targets MMC5 despite prerequisite binary retaining an mmc3-clang name. MIT engine/tools; no original game assets included. id Software game data must be provided by the user. Public ModdingWiki/Omnispeak are stated behavior references.

### Identity (reviewed)

Java implementation of the Xerox Mesa machine architecture in two machine profiles: Draco emulates the 6085 Daybreak/Dove and Duchess reconstructs the Guam environment used by GVWin. Implements Mesa CPU, virtual memory, traps, disk/floppy formats, display/input, networking and execution debugging.
- https://github.com/devhawala/dwarf/blob/c264af5e37f89d7aa0eec968aa23818bf5a89837/readme.md
- https://github.com/devhawala/dwarf/blob/c264af5e37f89d7aa0eec968aa23818bf5a89837/.classpath

- https://github.com/devhawala/dwarf/commit/c264af5e37f89d7aa0eec968aa23818bf5a89837

### Classification (reviewed)

Mesa instruction-level implementation, explicitly not a microcoded hardware emulator; alternate Lisp/Smalltalk/Cedar microcodes cannot run. Author-tested Linux Mint and Windows 7/10. Historic system disks are separate subject materials. BSD-3-Clause root and CPU header. Historical Xerox system media do not acquire this licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

C23 Apollo Domain family emulator with Motorola 68020/68030/68040 machine profiles, storage controllers and a reconstructed proprietary Apollo token ring. Implements ring framing/MAC/PHY and firmware-visible devices; includes differential/oracle tooling and a substantial test suite.
- https://github.com/GavinMGlynn/emulator_apollo/blob/6c57bfd640b2eef51a425194c8ab209a60a5b4cb/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Upstream reports Domain/OS SR10.2/10.3/10.4 login boots and two-node networking; not independently verified. Six of twelve models lack runnable oracle, and numerous timing/readings remain explicitly provisional. Latest commit reports upstream tests; those are not tests performed here. MIT core; GPL-2.0-or-later MAME oracle is kept outside core and never linked according to build-boundary checks. Musashi read as a reference. Firmware/media/manuals excluded and separately obtained.

### Identity (reviewed)

Rust/C++ emulator of original SGI IRIS Indigo IP12, centered on the MIPS R3000A with instruction decoding, MMU/cache/coprocessors, PIC1/HPC1/INT2 devices, SCSI, graphics, network and a Qt debugger UI.
- https://github.com/rickgcn/sgi-emu/blob/421936c77baaee5ec47406581eb670b95c744126/README.md
- https://github.com/rickgcn/sgi-emu/blob/421936c77baaee5ec47406581eb670b95c744126/Cargo.toml

- https://github.com/rickgcn/sgi-emu/commit/421936c77baaee5ec47406581eb670b95c744126

### Classification (reviewed)

Upstream reports IRIX 5.3 desktop and NetBSD boots. Work-in-progress: graphics/SCSI partial and audio unimplemented. IP20/IP22/IP24/IP32 are future possibilities, not supported machines. GPL-3.0-or-later in Cargo metadata; root GPLv3 text. IRIX media not distributed and original firmware may be needed.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable high-level execution environment for Tapwave Zodiac native ARM applications. Uses Unicorn for ARM and 68k launch code while reconstructing the 612-entry TwGlue/Palm OS interface, graphics, input, sound, VFS and application callbacks. This is an application-class emulator, not a wrapper for one game.
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/ecliptic/blob/53f22dea3002847e602e85cc2f619f595be3c6ad/README.md
- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/ecliptic/blob/53f22dea3002847e602e85cc2f619f595be3c6ad/CMakeLists.txt

- https://github.com/vs-sr-dev/ecliptic/commit/53f22dea3002847e602e85cc2f619f595be3c6ad

### Classification (reviewed)

HLE, not full-device emulation; optional ROM supplies system databases/fonts. Unknown glue slots log or stop in strict mode. Per-title status remains upstream reports, not independent compatibility testing. MIT implementation; Unicorn/dependencies and Palm ROM/fonts/game data retain separate terms.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Palm OS utility that shifts the system calendar epoch/window, converts stored dates with backup, reinstalls after reset and patches date conversion/formatting/select-day APIs on 68k Palm OS 3.5–4.x and ARM Palm OS 5. Source contains separate 68k installer and native ARM patch implementation.
- https://github.com/User7142/palm-datefix/blob/0e161126bb5c5fc8f68ae47876e23b055b148aea/README.md
- https://github.com/User7142/palm-datefix/blob/0e161126bb5c5fc8f68ae47876e23b055b148aea/Makefile
- https://github.com/User7142/palm-datefix/blob/0e161126bb5c5fc8f68ae47876e23b055b148aea/LICENSE

- https://github.com/User7142/palm-datefix/blob/0e161126bb5c5fc8f68ae47876e23b055b148aea/src/datefix.c
- https://github.com/User7142/palm-datefix/commit/0e161126bb5c5fc8f68ae47876e23b055b148aea

### Classification (reviewed)

Beta. Upstream verifies Tungsten T3/m515/emulators; not verified here. Changes stored data, truncates dates before chosen window and requires HotSync/SD backups. Does not fix every app drawing its own dates. Default window is 1932–2059, not 2099. MIT root licence; Palm SDK and original OS remain separately licensed.

### Identity (reviewed)

C# reconstruction of Xerox PARC PUP/Interim File Server protocols: BreathOfLife network boot, EFTP, FTP, CopyDisk disk imaging, time/name service, routing and mail. Serves Alto emulators or real Alto machines using UDP/raw Ethernet and optional BeagleBone interface.
- https://github.com/jdersch/IFS/blob/2c781a83620778fb36ddc637a0c038864ece700a/README.md
- https://github.com/jdersch/IFS/blob/2c781a83620778fb36ddc637a0c038864ece700a/PUP/IFS.csproj

- https://github.com/jdersch/IFS/commit/2c781a83620778fb36ddc637a0c038864ece700a

### Classification (reviewed)

Reusable historical protocol service, distinct from an Alto CPU emulator. README warns all files globally readable and passwords plaintext; unsuitable for mission/security-critical use. README runtime claims and legacy csproj differ and have not been reconciled by a build. AGPL-3.0-or-later stated in implementation header; AGPLv3 root text. Bundled pcap/.NET components have their own terms.

### Identity (reviewed)

Java mainframe programming environment implementing HLASM-compatible macro assembly, linkage editing, zCOBOL compilation and a runtime emulator with QSAM/VSAM/CICS services. Substantial assembler, processor and service implementations, macro libraries and regression tests exist.

### Identity (reviewed)

Java Swing integrated development and simulation environment for IBM 1401. Combines source editing, Autocoder invocation, SIMH process control, execution/breakpoints, memory/console/printout views and timer/UI support; original frontend source is maintained separately from bundled external engines.

### Identity (reviewed)

Python implementation of IBM 1620 Model 1 decimal CPU with 20,000-digit default memory, flag bits, instruction execution, card-file and typewriter I/O, CMEM loading and built-in debugger. Supports varied historic programs and compiler/assembler workflows rather than a game-specific wrapper.
- https://github.com/mdoege/Py1620/blob/ccf1b3d827d5f6da50b9c999e38e86c48d32c250/README.md
- https://github.com/mdoege/Py1620/blob/ccf1b3d827d5f6da50b9c999e38e86c48d32c250/py1620.py

- https://github.com/mdoege/Py1620/commit/ccf1b3d827d5f6da50b9c999e38e86c48d32c250

### Classification (reviewed)

Limited Model1 with optional divide; indirect addressing and hardware floating point absent. It uses Python integer arithmetic instead of original in-memory addition/multiplication tables. Different implementation and Model1 scope from tracked retro-1620 Model2 browser emulator. No project-wide software licence found in root or inspected Python source. README image licences are not a code licence. Historical programs/compiler decks have separate provenance.

Source completeness, rights and AI attribution remain qualified. No upstream software executed; all earlier catalogue records preserved.
